  ▶ webhook transaction (25.7, 38.2 item 13)

    ✔ same event ID from another source is a different message (unique per organisation, source, event) (5.331186ms)
    ✔ a bad signature is 401, is not recorded, enqueues nothing and is counted (3.021992ms)
    ✔ malformed after a valid signature is 400; an unknown source is 404; an oversize body is 413; none stored (1.392814ms)
    ✔ Google push: valid token accepted; a repeated message number is a duplicate; the next number is new; bad token is 401 (7.887203ms)
    ✔ if the transaction fails the vendor gets 503 and neither the message nor the job is stored (6.989749ms)

▶ notifications on PostgreSQL 17 (BE-06, API side)
  ▶ templates (22.13, RA-R1-05, BD-10)
    ✔ a template binding a service, diagnosis, medicine or clinician name cannot be saved - service layer and database (67.344546ms)
    ✔ render check then publish: needs the check, needs every configured language, publishes the whole version, retires the old one (216.217456ms)
    ✔ only the admin realm writes templates; staff and patients read them (T-CONF); anonymous reads none (10.056204ms)
  ✔ templates (22.13, RA-R1-05, BD-10) (294.422495ms)
  ▶ send, dedupe, cancel (22.13, 24, BR-08)
    ✔ send binds allowlisted values only: one queued row per channel, a job per row in the same transaction, no address, no text (73.307466ms)
    ✔ free text, unknown names, a service or clinician field, bad links and condition-revealing location names never reach a notification (TC-NT-06) (10.655529ms)
    ✔ dedupe keys prevent double sends: a repeat creates nothing and queues nothing, also under concurrency (100.780388ms)
    ✔ a rolled-back business transaction leaves no notification and no job; a delayed send waits for send_after (28.033234ms)
    ✔ cancel by dedupe-key prefix cancels queued rows of that resource only; sent rows are final (103.684931ms)
    ✔ in-app notices: a patient lists only their own sent notices and marks them read once (59.723702ms)
  ✔ send, dedupe, cancel (22.13, 24, BR-08) (974.693226ms)
  ▶ row-level security and grants
    ✔ app_api: patients see only their own in-app rows; staff (google) and admin see no patient notification; anonymous sees nothing (9.839447ms)
    ✔ inserts: a patient only for themselves, queued only; admin realm only to principals; a worker needs its patients scope (13.428142ms)
    ✔ the application role cannot change content, delete, or touch receipts; receipts and reads are append-only (12.234682ms)
    ✔ table rules: dedupe key without the recipient, recipient kind consistent, values on the allowlist, forward-only status (11.743941ms)
  ✔ row-level security and grants (47.672554ms)
  ▶ merge and export participants (22.16, 24, SBR-16)
    ✔ repointPatient: R rows move to the survivor, K rows stay, counts add up for notifications, receipts and reads; scope is enforced (53.343908ms)
    ✔ exportPart returns the patient's notifications, receipts and reads - no address and no message text exist to leak (4.708437ms)
    ✔ the participants register with the kernel registries once (0.51652ms)
  ✔ merge and export participants (22.16, 24, SBR-16) (58.775391ms)
✔ notifications on PostgreSQL 17 (BE-06, API side) (5124.888646ms)
